# MCP Tools Reference

_Generated by `pnpm docs:tools` from the live catalog — do not edit by hand._

**296 tools**: 263 supported, 21 partial (documented approximation), 6 require an external AI provider, 6 unsupported (return `UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION` with an alternative).

## Common output schema

Every tool returns one text content block containing JSON, plus image blocks when it renders a preview:

```json
{ "ok": true, "tool": "<name>", "source": "photoshop | mock | server", "data": { "...tool specific..." }, "warnings": ["..."] }
```

Errors set `isError: true` and return `{ "ok": false, "tool": "<name>", "error": { "code": "PHOTOSHOP_ERROR", "message": "...", "details": {} } }`.
Codes: VALIDATION_ERROR, PHOTOSHOP_NOT_CONNECTED, PHOTOSHOP_ERROR, BRIDGE_TIMEOUT, CANCELLED, NO_ACTIVE_DOCUMENT, LAYER_NOT_FOUND, DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND, PATH_NOT_ALLOWED, FILE_EXISTS, FILE_NOT_FOUND, CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED, UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION, PROVIDER_NOT_CONFIGURED, PROVIDER_ERROR, LIMIT_EXCEEDED, WORKFLOW_FAILED, INTERNAL_ERROR.

**Permissions.** Read-only tools need nothing beyond a reachable Photoshop (COM on Windows, AppleScript on macOS). Write tools need an open document (unless they create one). Tools that touch files need the path inside `ALLOWED_PATHS`. Destructive tools need `confirm: true`. AI-provider tools need provider credentials and network access (`openWorldHint`).


## Status

### `photoshop_status`

Detect installed Photoshop versions, bridge readiness (COM on Windows, AppleScript on macOS) and whether Photoshop is running. With probe: true also queries the running application (version, open documents) via a real script call. Call first.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `ps.info`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "probe": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true,
      "description": "Execute a status script inside Photoshop (may launch it if auto-launch is enabled)"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"photoshop_status","arguments":{}}`

## 1. Document management

### `create_document`

Create a new Photoshop document (pixels).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "default": "Untitled"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 300000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 300000
    },
    "resolution": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10000,
      "default": 72
    },
    "mode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "rgb",
        "cmyk",
        "grayscale",
        "lab",
        "bitmap"
      ],
      "default": "rgb"
    },
    "bitDepth": {
      "type": "number",
      "enum": [
        8,
        16,
        32
      ],
      "default": 8
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "white",
        "black",
        "transparent",
        "backgroundColor"
      ],
      "default": "white"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "width",
    "height"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_document","arguments":{"width":1,"height":1}}`

### `open_document`

Open an image or PSD from an allowed directory.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.open`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"open_document","arguments":{"path":"designs/example.png"}}`

### `close_document`

Close a document. save: 'yes' saves first (needs an existing path); 'no' discards unsaved changes and requires confirm: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.close`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "save": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "yes",
        "no"
      ],
      "default": "yes"
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "Must be true for destructive operations, after asking the user"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"close_document","arguments":{}}`

### `save_document_as`

Save the document to a new file and make it the document's file (PSD/PSB/TIFF keep layers). Never overwrites unless overwrite: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.saveAs`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "psd",
        "psb",
        "png",
        "jpg",
        "tiff",
        "pdf",
        "webp",
        "gif",
        "bmp"
      ],
      "default": "psd"
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    },
    "quality": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 12,
      "description": "JPEG quality 0-12"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"save_document_as","arguments":{"path":"designs/example.png"}}`

### `resize_document`

Image Size: resample the whole document. Give width and/or height (aspect kept if one is omitted) or percent.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.resizeImage`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 300000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 300000
    },
    "percent": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10000
    },
    "resample": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "bicubicAutomatic",
        "bicubic",
        "bicubicSharper",
        "bicubicSmoother",
        "bilinear",
        "nearestNeighbor",
        "preserveDetails",
        "none"
      ],
      "default": "bicubicAutomatic"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"resize_document","arguments":{}}`

### `change_color_mode`

Convert color mode. Converting can flatten adjustment layers or change colors; requires confirm: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.mode`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "mode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "rgb",
        "cmyk",
        "grayscale",
        "lab",
        "bitmap",
        "indexed"
      ]
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "Must be true for destructive operations, after asking the user"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "mode"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"change_color_mode","arguments":{"mode":"rgb"}}`

## 2. Layer management

### `get_layers`

Layer tree top-to-bottom: id, name, kind, visibility, opacity, blend mode, lock, bounds, masks, text summary.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.list`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
```

### `create_layer`

Create an empty pixel layer or a group; optionally fill it with a color.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.create`, `layer.fill`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "kind": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "pixel",
        "group"
      ],
      "default": "pixel"
    },

    "blendMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "normal",
        "dissolve",
        "darken",
        "multiply",
        "colorBurn",
        "linearBurn",
        "darkerColor",
        "lighten",
        "screen",
        "colorDodge",
        "linearDodge",
        "lighterColor",
        "overlay",
        "softLight",
        "hardLight",
        "vividLight",
        "linearLight",
        "pinLight",
        "hardMix",
        "difference",
        "exclusion",
        "subtract",
        "divide",
        "hue",
        "saturation",
        "color",
        "luminosity"
      ]
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "description": "Opacity 0-100"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `align_layers`

Align layers (or groups) by an edge or center: to the canvas, to the bounding box of the layers themselves (default for 2+ layers), or to a reference layer. Moves only; sizes and order are untouched.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.list`, `doc.info`, `layer.translateMany`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layers": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "anyOf": [
          {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "id": {
                "type": "integer",
                "exclusiveMinimum": 0
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "id"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "name": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 255
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "name"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          }
        ],
        "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 500
    },
    "align": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "left",
        "centerHorizontal",
        "right",
        "top",
        "centerVertical",
        "bottom"
      ]
    },
    "to": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "canvas",
        "layers",
        "reference"
      ],
      "description": "Default: canvas for one layer, layers (their combined bounds) for several"
    },
    "reference": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Key layer to align to (with to: reference); it does not move"
    },
    "margin": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0,
      "description": "Inset from the target edge (edge alignments only)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layers",
    "align"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"align_layers","arguments":{"layers":[],"align":"left"}}`

```json
            }

        },
        {

            }
```

Example: `{"name":"get_layer_bounds","arguments":{}}`

## 3. Text & typography

### `add_text`

Create a live (editable) text layer. (x, y) is the text anchor: baseline of the first line; for center/right alignment x is the center/right edge. Give box {width,height} for paragraph text.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `text.create`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "text": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 20000
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "font": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200,
      "description": "PostScript font name, e.g. ArialMT, Arial-BoldMT, TimesNewRomanPSMT, MyriadPro-Regular. Missing fonts are reported, never silently assumed.",
      "default": "ArialMT"
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.1,
      "maximum": 5000,
      "default": 48,
      "description": "Size in px"
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C",
      "default": "#000000"
    },
    "alignment": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "left",
        "center",
        "right",
        "justify"
      ],
      "default": "left"
    },
    "tracking": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -1000,
      "maximum": 10000,
      "default": 0
    },
    "leading": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.1,
      "maximum": 5000
    },
    "box": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "width": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 1
        },
        "height": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 1
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "width",
        "height"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "text",
    "x",
    "y"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"add_text","arguments":{"text":"Example","x":100,"y":100}}`

```json
    "scale": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 5000,

    },
    "rotate": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -360,
      "maximum": 360
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
        "spread": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 100,
          "default": 0
        },
        "blendMode": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "normal",
            "dissolve",
            "darken",
            "multiply",
            "colorBurn",
            "linearBurn",
            "darkerColor",
            "lighten",
            "screen",
            "colorDodge",
            "linearDodge",
            "lighterColor",
            "overlay",
            "softLight",
            "hardLight",
            "vividLight",
            "linearLight",
            "pinLight",
            "hardMix",
            "difference",
            "exclusion",
            "subtract",
            "divide",
            "hue",
            "saturation",
            "color",
            "luminosity"
          ],
          "default": "screen"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "default": {}
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_text_glow","arguments":{}}`

### `convert_text_to_shape`

Convert text to a vector shape layer (no longer editable as text). Requires confirm: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `text.toShape`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "Must be true for destructive operations, after asking the user"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"convert_text_to_shape","arguments":{}}`

### `create_typography_design`

Typographic composition on a new canvas: headline (+ optional subhead) with style-driven fonts, colors and effects.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `fill.gradient`, `text.create`, `layer.effects`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "headline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "subhead": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 300
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 1920
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 1080
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "luxury",
        "minimal",
        "bold",
        "cinematic",
        "vintage",
        "tech",
        "nature",
        "playful",
        "corporate"
      ],
      "default": "bold"
    },
    "bevel": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "style": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "innerBevel",
            "outerBevel",
            "emboss",
            "pillowEmboss"
          ],
          "default": "innerBevel"
        },
        "depth": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 1,
          "maximum": 1000,
          "default": 100
        },
        "size": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 250,
          "default": 5
        },
        "soften": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 16,
          "default": 0
        },
        "angle": {
          "type": "number",
          "default": 120
        },
        "altitude": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 90,
          "default": 30
        },
        "direction": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "up",
            "down"
          ],
          "default": "up"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "useActiveDocument": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "headline"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_typography_design","arguments":{"headline":"Example"}}`

## 4. Shapes & graphic design

### `create_rectangle`

Vector rectangle shape layer (optional corner radius, fill, stroke).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `shape.rect`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "bounds": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "left": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "top": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "right": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "bottom": {
          "type": "number"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "left",
        "top",
        "right",
        "bottom"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
    },
    "fill": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Fill color (default #000000)"
    },
    "stroke": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "color": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0.1,
          "maximum": 250,
          "default": 2
        },
        "position": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "outside",
            "inside",
            "center"
          ],
          "default": "outside"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "color"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "radius": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 10000,
      "default": 0
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "description": "Opacity 0-100"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "bounds"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_rectangle","arguments":{"bounds":{"left":0,"top":0,"right":200,"bottom":100}}}`

Vector polygon shape layer from points, or a regular polygon from center/radius/sides.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },

          ],

        },
        {

            }

          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0.1,
          "maximum": 250,
          "default": 2
        },
        "position": {
          "type": "string",

          ],
          "default": "outside"
        }
      },
      "required": [

      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,

      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,

}
```

### `create_gradient`

Gradient fill layer (editable). Covers the canvas, or only `bounds` (masked).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `fill.gradient`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "gradient": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "stops": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "color": {
                "type": "string",
                "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
              },
              "location": {
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100
              },
              "opacity": {
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100,
                "default": 100,
                "description": "Stop opacity (transparency stop at the same location)"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "color",
              "location"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          "minItems": 2,
          "maxItems": 16
        },
        "angle": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -360,
          "maximum": 360,
          "default": 90
        },
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "linear",
            "radial",
            "angle",
            "reflected",
            "diamond"
          ],
          "default": "linear"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "stops"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "bounds": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "left": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "top": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "right": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "bottom": {
          "type": "number"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "left",
        "top",
        "right",
        "bottom"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "description": "Opacity 0-100"
    },
    "blendMode": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "gradient"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_gradient","arguments":{"gradient":{}}}`

### `create_pattern`

Procedural pattern (dots, grid, stripes, checker, diagonal) drawn as one pixel layer.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.create`, `select.rect`, `select.ellipse`, `edit.fillSelection`, `select.none`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "kind": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "dots",
        "grid",
        "stripes",
        "checker",
        "diagonal"
      ],
      "default": "dots"
    },
    "spacing": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 4,
      "maximum": 2000,
      "default": 40
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 2000,
      "default": 8
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C",
      "default": "#000000"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 30
    },
    "bounds": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "left": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "top": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "right": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "bottom": {
          "type": "number"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "left",
        "top",
        "right",
        "bottom"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name",
      "default": "Pattern"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `create_banner`

Wide web/hero banner. Builds fully editable layers (fill layers, vector shapes, live text, smart-object image) and returns every step.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `fill.gradient`, `shape.rect`, `shape.ellipse`, `text.create`, `image.place`, `layer.effects`, `filter.apply`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "headline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "subhead": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 300
    },
    "body": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 1000
    },
    "cta": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 60
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "luxury",
        "minimal",
        "bold",
        "cinematic",
        "vintage",
        "tech",
        "nature",
        "playful",
        "corporate"
      ],
      "default": "bold"
    },
    "colors": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
      },
      "maxItems": 2,
      "description": "Override primary/accent colors"
    },
    "imagePath": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Optional hero image inside an allowed directory"
    },
    "removeBackground": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "useActiveDocument": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Draw into the active document instead of creating one"
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "instagram_post",
        "instagram_portrait",
        "instagram_story",
        "facebook_post",
        "facebook_cover",
        "twitter_post",
        "twitter_header",
        "linkedin_post",
        "linkedin_banner",
        "pinterest_pin",
        "youtube_thumbnail",
        "youtube_banner",
        "web_banner",
        "leaderboard",
        "medium_rectangle",
        "hd",
        "4k",
        "poster_a3",
        "poster_18x24",
        "flyer_letter",
        "flyer_a4",
        "magazine_cover",
        "album_cover",
        "ecommerce"
      ],
      "default": "web_banner"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "headline"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_banner","arguments":{"headline":"Example"}}`

```json
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "useActiveDocument": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Draw into the active document instead of creating one"
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "instagram_post",
        "instagram_portrait",
        "instagram_story",
        "facebook_post",
        "facebook_cover",
        "twitter_post",
        "twitter_header",
        "linkedin_post",
        "linkedin_banner",
        "pinterest_pin",
        "youtube_thumbnail",
        "youtube_banner",
        "web_banner",
        "leaderboard",
        "medium_rectangle",
        "hd",
        "4k",
        "poster_a3",
        "poster_18x24",
        "flyer_letter",
        "flyer_a4",
        "magazine_cover",
        "album_cover",
        "ecommerce"
      ],
      "default": "magazine_cover"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "headline"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_magazine_cover","arguments":{"headline":"Example"}}`

### `create_album_cover`

Square album cover art layout. Builds fully editable layers (fill layers, vector shapes, live text, smart-object image) and returns every step.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `fill.gradient`, `shape.rect`, `shape.ellipse`, `text.create`, `image.place`, `layer.effects`, `filter.apply`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "headline": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "subhead": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 300
    },
    "body": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 1000
    },
    "cta": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 60
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "luxury",
        "minimal",
        "bold",
        "cinematic",
        "vintage",
        "tech",
        "nature",
        "playful",
        "corporate"
      ],
      "default": "bold"
    },
    "colors": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
      },
      "maxItems": 2,
      "description": "Override primary/accent colors"
    },
    "imagePath": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Optional hero image inside an allowed directory"
    },
    "removeBackground": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "useActiveDocument": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Draw into the active document instead of creating one"
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "instagram_post",
        "instagram_portrait",
        "instagram_story",
        "facebook_post",
        "facebook_cover",
        "twitter_post",
        "twitter_header",
        "linkedin_post",
        "linkedin_banner",
        "pinterest_pin",
        "youtube_thumbnail",
        "youtube_banner",
        "web_banner",
        "leaderboard",
        "medium_rectangle",
        "hd",
        "4k",
        "poster_a3",
        "poster_18x24",
        "flyer_letter",
        "flyer_a4",
        "magazine_cover",
        "album_cover",
        "ecommerce"
      ],
      "default": "album_cover"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "headline"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_album_cover","arguments":{"headline":"Example"}}`

## 5. Image operations

### `import_image`

Open an image file as a new document.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.open`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"import_image","arguments":{"path":"designs/example.png"}}`

### `place_image`

Place an image file into the document as an embedded smart object, optionally fitted into a box (x, y, width, height).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `image.place`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "fit": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "contain",
        "cover",
        "stretch",
        "none"
      ],
      "default": "contain"
    },
    "linked": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"place_image","arguments":{"path":"designs/example.png"}}`

### `crop_image`

Crop the canvas to bounds (pixels outside are deleted unless layers extend beyond). Requires confirm: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.crop`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "bounds": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "left": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "top": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "right": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "bottom": {
          "type": "number"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "left",
        "top",
        "right",
        "bottom"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "Must be true for destructive operations, after asking the user"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "bounds"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"crop_image","arguments":{"bounds":{"left":0,"top":0,"right":200,"bottom":100}}}`

### `rotate_image`

Rotate the whole canvas (degrees, clockwise).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.rotate`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "angle": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -360,
      "maximum": 360
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "angle"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"rotate_image","arguments":{"angle":100}}`

### `flip_image`

Flip the canvas, or one layer when target is 'layer'.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.flip`, `layer.transform`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "direction": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "horizontal",
        "vertical"
      ]
    },
    "target": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "document",
        "layer"
      ],
      "default": "document"
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "direction"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"flip_image","arguments":{"direction":"horizontal"}}`

### `resize_image`

Resample the document (same as resize_document).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.resizeImage`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",

            }

          ],

        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {

            }
          },

}
```

### `transform_image`

Free transform a layer: scale (%), rotate, skew, move, flip. Smart objects keep full quality.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.transform`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    },
    "scaleX": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.1,
      "maximum": 10000
    },
    "scaleY": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0.1,
      "maximum": 10000
    },
    "angle": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -360,
      "maximum": 360
    },
    "skewX": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -89,
      "maximum": 89
    },
    "skewY": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -89,
      "maximum": 89
    },
    "dx": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "dy": {
      "type": "number"
    },
    "flip": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "horizontal",
        "vertical"
      ]
    },
    "anchor": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "center",
        "topLeft",
        "bottom",
        "top"
      ],
      "default": "center"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"transform_image","arguments":{}}`

### `convert_image`

Convert an image file to another format: opens it, saves a copy, closes it without changes.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.open`, `doc.saveAs`, `doc.close`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "input": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "output": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "psd",
        "psb",
        "png",
        "jpg",
        "tiff",
        "pdf",
        "webp",
        "gif",
        "bmp"
      ]
    },
    "quality": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "input",
    "output",
    "format"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"convert_image","arguments":{"input":"designs/example.png","output":"designs/example.png","format":"psd"}}`

### `get_image_metadata`

File metadata from the header: format, dimensions, bit depth, channels, color mode (PSD), size, modified time.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    }
  },
```

### `create_image_contact_sheet`

Contact sheet: grid of thumbnails (smart objects) with file-name captions from a list of files or a folder.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `image.place`, `text.create`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "paths": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 400
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "columns": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 20,
      "default": 4
    },
    "cellSize": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 32,
      "maximum": 4000,
      "default": 400
    },
    "gap": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 20
    },
    "captions": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "string",
      "default": "#FFFFFF"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_image_contact_sheet","arguments":{}}`

### `select_color_range`

Select > Color Range using a preset range (reds…magentas, highlights, midtones, shadows, skin tones).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `select.colorRange`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [

          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],

    },

      "type": "string",

        "yellows",
        "greens",
        "cyans",
        "blues",
        "magentas",
        "highlights",

        "shadows",

        "add",
        "subtract",
        "intersect"
      ],
      "default": "new"
    }

}
```

```json
                  "type": "number"
                },
                "top": {
                  "type": "number"
                },
                "right": {
                  "type": "number"
                },
                "bottom": {
                  "type": "number"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "left",
                "top",
                "right",
                "bottom"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false,
              "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "shape",
            "bounds"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "shape": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "polygon"
            },
            "points": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                  "x": {
                    "type": "number"
                  },
                  "y": {
                    "type": "number"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "x",
                  "y"
                ],
                "additionalProperties": false
              },
              "minItems": 3,
              "maxItems": 500
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "shape",
            "points"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Image region in document pixels"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `replace_background`

Mask out the background, then add a new background layer below the subject (solid color, gradient or image).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `select.subject`, `mask.create`, `fill.solid`, `fill.gradient`, `image.place`, `layer.arrange`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
    },
    "gradient": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "stops": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "color": {
                "type": "string",
                "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
              },
              "location": {
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100
              },
              "opacity": {
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100,
                "default": 100,
                "description": "Stop opacity (transparency stop at the same location)"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "color",
              "location"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          "minItems": 2,
          "maxItems": 16
        },
        "angle": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -360,
          "maximum": 360,
          "default": 90
        },
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "linear",
            "radial",
            "angle",
            "reflected",
            "diamond"
          ],
          "default": "linear"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "stops"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "imagePath": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

```json
                "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
              },
              "location": {
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100
              },
              "opacity": {
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100,
                "default": 100,
                "description": "Stop opacity (transparency stop at the same location)"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "color",
              "location"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          "minItems": 2,
          "maxItems": 16
        },
        "angle": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -360,
          "maximum": 360,
          "default": 90
        },
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "linear",
            "radial",
            "angle",
            "reflected",
            "diamond"
          ],
          "default": "linear"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "stops"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "imagePath": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_custom_background","arguments":{}}`

### `extend_background`

Extend the canvas on any side and fill the new area with Content-Aware Fill on a flattened copy layer.

- **Status:** partial — Content-Aware Fill works well for textures/skies; for complex scenes use generative_expand with a configured Firefly provider.
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.info`, `doc.resizeCanvas`, `layer.create`, `select.rect`, `select.invert`, `edit.contentAwareFill`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [

              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }

          "required": [

          ],
          "additionalProperties": false

        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {

              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."

      "type": "integer",

      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 0

        {
```

## 8. Photo retouching

### `smooth_skin`

Skin smoothing: surface-blurred smart copy masked to skin tones (Color Range), blended by strength.

- **Status:** partial — Heuristic, non-destructive layer stack built from Photoshop filters/adjustments; it is not a Neural Filter. Review with capture_preview.
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.duplicate`, `layer.smartObject`, `filter.apply`, `select.colorRange`, `mask.create`, `layer.props`

- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `ps.analyze`, `adjust.layer`

### `remove_scratches`

Remove scratches: Dust & Scratches smart filter on a copy, optionally masked to regions; region list also gets Content-Aware Fill.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `filter.apply`, `edit.contentAwareFill`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    },
    "radius": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 50,
      "default": 3
    },
    "threshold": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 255,
      "default": 12
    },
    "regions": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "left": {
            "type": "number"
          },
          "top": {
            "type": "number"
          },
          "right": {
            "type": "number"
          },
          "bottom": {
            "type": "number"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "left",
          "top",
          "right",
          "bottom"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
      },
      "maxItems": 100
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

### `colorize_photo`

Tint/tone a black-and-white photo with a gradient map in Color blend mode (sepia, warm, cool or custom 3 colors).

- **Status:** partial — Photoshop's Colorize Neural Filter is not scriptable; this applies an editable toning map, not AI colorization.
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `adjust.layer`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "sepia",
        "warm",
        "cool",
        "custom"
      ],
      "default": "sepia"
    },
    "colors": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "minItems": 3,
      "maxItems": 3,
      "description": "shadow, mid, highlight hex (custom)"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 70
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"colorize_photo","arguments":{}}`

- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `adjust.layer`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Insert the adjustment layer above this layer (default: active)"
    },
    "clip": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Clip the adjustment to the layer below (affects only that layer)"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255,
      "description": "Layer name"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "description": "Opacity 0-100"
    },
    "blendMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "normal",
        "dissolve",
        "darken",
        "multiply",
        "colorBurn",
        "linearBurn",
        "darkerColor",
        "lighten",
        "screen",
        "colorDodge",
        "linearDodge",
        "lighterColor",
        "overlay",
        "softLight",
        "hardLight",
        "vividLight",
        "linearLight",
        "pinLight",
        "hardMix",
        "difference",
        "exclusion",
        "subtract",
        "divide",
        "hue",
        "saturation",
        "color",
        "luminosity"
      ]
    },
    "brightness": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": -150,
      "maximum": 150
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "brightness"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

```json
        "bleach_bypass",
        "noir",
        "warm_film",
        "cool_night"
      ],
      "default": "teal_orange"
    },
    "strength": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 70
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"apply_cinematic_color_grade","arguments":{}}`

### `match_colors`

Match the color/tonal statistics of a target layer (or document) to a reference document or layer using measured channel means → clipped Levels per channel.

- **Status:** partial — Statistical match (mean per channel and luminance), not Photoshop's Match Color dialog.
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `ps.analyze`, `adjust.layer`

```json
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    },
```
```json
    "settings": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "color": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C",
```
```json
          "default": "#000000"
        },
        "opacity": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 100,
          "default": 60
        },
        "angle": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": -360,
          "maximum": 360,
          "default": 120
        },
        "distance": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 30000,
          "default": 10
        },
        "size": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 250,
          "default": 15
        },
        "spread": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 0,
          "maximum": 100,
          "default": 0
        },
        "blendMode": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "normal",
            "dissolve",
            "darken",
            "multiply",
            "colorBurn",
            "linearBurn",
            "darkerColor",
            "lighten",
            "screen",
            "colorDodge",
            "linearDodge",
            "lighterColor",
            "overlay",
            "softLight",
            "hardLight",
            "vividLight",
            "linearLight",
            "pinLight",
            "hardMix",
            "difference",
            "exclusion",
            "subtract",
            "divide",
            "hue",
            "saturation",
            "color",
            "luminosity"
          ],
          "default": "multiply"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "default": {}
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
