## 11. Photo compositing

### `combine_images`

Combine images into one document: stacked layers, side-by-side, or a grid. Each image is a smart object layer.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `image.place`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "paths": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "minItems": 2,
      "maxItems": 50
    },
    "layout": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "stack",
        "horizontal",
        "vertical",
        "grid"
      ],
      "default": "horizontal"
    },
    "cell": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 32,
      "maximum": 10000,
      "default": 1000
    },
    "gap": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1000,
      "default": 0
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C",
      "default": "#FFFFFF"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "paths"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"combine_images","arguments":{"paths":[]}}`

### `create_photo_collage`

Photo collage: each photo fills (cover) a grid cell, clipped to a cell shape, with gaps and background.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `shape.rect`, `image.place`, `layer.clip`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "paths": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "minItems": 2,
      "maxItems": 60
    },
    "columns": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 12,
      "default": 3
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 100,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 3000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 100,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "gap": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 20
    },
    "radius": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 0
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C",
      "default": "#FFFFFF"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "paths"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_photo_collage","arguments":{"paths":[]}}`

### `create_photo_composite`

Composite a subject photo onto a background photo: subject cut out (mask), scaled into a box, color-matched to the background, with contact shadow.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `image.place`, `select.subject`, `mask.create`, `ps.analyze`, `adjust.layer`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "background": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "subject": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 1333
    },
    "subjectBox": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "x": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 1
        },
        "height": {
          "type": "number",
          "minimum": 1
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y",
        "width",
        "height"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "shadow": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "matchColors": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "background",
    "subject"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_photo_composite","arguments":{"background":"Example","subject":"Example"}}`

### `create_surreal_artwork`

Surreal mood: warm film grade, dreamy glow, soft haze. Works on the active document with editable layers.

- **Status:** partial — Builds mood (grade, light, atmosphere, vignette) from layers; it does not generate new scene content unless you place assets or use generate_image.
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `adjust.layer`, `fill.gradient`, `shape.ellipse`, `filter.apply`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "strength": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 70
    },
    "lightColor": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
    },
    "elements": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 20,
      "description": "Optional extra images to place (smart objects)"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_surreal_artwork","arguments":{}}`

### `create_product_composite`

Product shot: cut out the product, place on a gradient set, add reflection, contact shadow and rim glow.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `fill.gradient`, `image.place`, `select.subject`, `mask.create`, `layer.effects`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "product": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "backdrop": {
      "type": "array",
      "minItems": 2,
      "maxItems": 2,
      "items": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
        }
      ],
      "default": [
        "#2A2A2A",
        "#050505"
      ]
    },
    "reflection": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "glow": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Hex color, e.g. #1A2B3C"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "product"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_product_composite","arguments":{"product":"Example"}}`

### `create_realistic_shadow`

Contact/cast shadow from a layer's shape: filled silhouette below the layer, squashed, blurred (smart filter) and multiplied.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.create`, `select.layerPixels`, `edit.fillSelection`, `layer.transform`, `filter.apply`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 50
    },
    "blur": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 500,
      "default": 15
    },
    "squash": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 100,
      "default": 30,
      "description": "Vertical scale % of the silhouette"
    },
    "offsetY": {
      "type": "number",
      "default": 0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## 12. Digital art

### `create_digital_painting`

Digital painting setup. Creates a 300ppi canvas with named layer groups and a palette.

- **Status:** partial — Sets up the document; brushwork must be done by the artist or by an external image provider.
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.create`, `layer.create`, `shape.rect`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 256,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 3000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 256,
      "maximum": 20000,
      "default": 2000
    },
    "title": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 120
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "luxury",
        "minimal",
        "bold",
        "cinematic",
        "vintage",
        "tech",
        "nature",
        "playful",
        "corporate"
      ],
      "default": "cinematic"
    },
    "subject": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 500
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_digital_painting","arguments":{}}`

```json
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 255,
      "default": 200
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_line_art","arguments":{}}`

### `apply_painting_effect`

Painterly look on a smart copy: oil (Oil Paint filter), watercolor (median + surface blur + texture) or poster (median + posterize).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `layer.duplicate`, `layer.smartObject`, `filter.apply`, `adjust.layer`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Layer reference {id} (preferred, from get_layers) or {name}. Omit to use the active layer."

    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "oil",
```

```json
{
  "type": "object",
```

Example: `{"name":"get_ai_capabilities","arguments":{}}`

### `generative_fill`

Generative fill of a region via Adobe Firefly Services (needs FIREFLY_CLIENT_ID/SECRET). Result is placed as a new masked layer over the region.

- **Status:** requires_provider — Photoshop's in-app Generative Fill is not exposed to scripting; this uploads a JPEG of the canvas and a mask to Firefly.
- **Kind:** external service
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `ps.preview`, `image.place`, `select.rect`, `mask.create`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "bounds": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "left": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "top": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "right": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "bottom": {
          "type": "number"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "left",
        "top",
        "right",
        "bottom"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "description": "Rectangle in document pixels (origin top-left)"
    },
    "prompt": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 1000
    },
    "shape": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "rect",
        "ellipse"
      ],
      "default": "rect"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "bounds"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"generative_fill","arguments":{"bounds":{"left":0,"top":0,"right":200,"bottom":100}}}`

### `generative_expand`

Expand the canvas and fill the new area via Firefly Services; with allowFallback the server uses Content-Aware extend_background instead when no provider is configured (reported).

- **Status:** requires_provider
- **Kind:** external service
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.resizeCanvas`, `image.place`, `edit.contentAwareFill`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "left": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "right": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "top": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "bottom": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "default": 0
    },
    "prompt": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 1000
    },
    "allowFallback": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### `run_action`

Play a recorded Photoshop action. Actions can do anything the user recorded (including saving or deleting), so this requires confirm: true.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** destructive (confirm: true)
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `action.run`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "set": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255
    },
    "action": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 255
    },
    "confirm": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "Must be true for destructive operations, after asking the user"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "set",
    "action"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"run_action","arguments":{"set":"Example","action":"Example"}}`

### `batch_process`

Batch: for each input file run tool steps (doc is injected), export a copy, close without saving. Source files are never changed.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.open`, `doc.saveAs`, `doc.close`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "files": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 10000
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "recursive": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "outputFolder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Output folder inside an allowed directory"
    },
    "suffix": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 40,
      "pattern": "^[\\w.-]*$",
      "default": ""
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Return an executionId immediately"
    },
    "steps": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][\\w-]{0,40}$",
            "description": "Step id; later steps may reference its result as {{steps.<id>.data.<path>}}"
          },
          "tool": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "args": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": {},

            "maxLength": 500
```

### `resize_batch`

Batch resize copies (width and/or height; aspect kept if one is omitted).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.open`, `doc.saveAs`, `doc.close`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "files": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 10000
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "recursive": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "outputFolder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "Output folder inside an allowed directory"
    },
    "suffix": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 40,
      "pattern": "^[\\w.-]*$",
      "default": ""
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Return an executionId immediately"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 30000
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "psd",
        "psb",
        "png",
        "jpg",
        "tiff",
        "pdf",
        "webp",
        "gif",
        "bmp"
      ],
      "default": "jpg"
    },
    "quality": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "outputFolder"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.open`, `text.create`, `image.place`, `doc.saveAs`, `doc.close`

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "files": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 10000
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "recursive": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },

      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,

      "maxLength": 40,

      "default": ""
```

### `plan_workflow`

Turn a natural-language design brief into an ordered plan of tool calls (nothing is executed). Review it, then run it with execute_workflow { steps }.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "brief": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 5,
      "maxLength": 4000
    },
    "assets": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 20
    },
    "outputFolder": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 200,
      "default": "designs"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "brief"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"plan_workflow","arguments":{"brief":"Example"}}`

### `create_automation_workflow`

Store a reusable workflow (named list of tool steps; later steps may reference {{steps.<id>.data...}} and {{vars.x}}).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "workflow": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "name": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 120
        },
        "description": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 2000
        },
        "steps": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "id": {
                "type": "string",
                "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][\\w-]{0,40}$",
                "description": "Step id; later steps may reference its result as {{steps.<id>.data.<path>}}"
              },
              "tool": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 100
              },
              "args": {
                "type": "object",
                "additionalProperties": {},
                "default": {}
              },
              "description": {
                "type": "string",
                "maxLength": 500
              },
              "continueOnError": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "default": false
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "tool"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          },
          "minItems": 1,
          "maxItems": 200
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "name",
        "steps"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "workflow"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_automation_workflow","arguments":{"workflow":{}}}`

### `execute_workflow`

Execute a workflow: inline steps, a stored workflow name, a built-in template, or a natural-language brief (planned then executed). Steps run in order and each result is verified; stops at the first failure. background: true returns an executionId.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "steps": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z][\\w-]{0,40}$",
            "description": "Step id; later steps may reference its result as {{steps.<id>.data.<path>}}"
          },
          "tool": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "args": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": {},
            "default": {}
          },
          "description": {
            "type": "string",
            "maxLength": 500
          },
          "continueOnError": {
            "type": "boolean",
            "default": false
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "tool"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      },
      "maxItems": 200
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 120
    },
    "template": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "portrait_retouch",
        "ecommerce_white",
        "photo_restoration",
        "cinematic_grade",
        "social_square"
      ]
    },
    "brief": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 4000
    },
    "assets": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 1,
        "maxLength": 1024,
        "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
      },
      "maxItems": 20
    },
    "vars": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {},
      "default": {}
    },
    "background": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"execute_workflow","arguments":{}}`

```json
      "type": "string",
```

```json
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "id"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

## 15. History & inspection

### `undo`

Step backward in history (1-50 steps).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `ps.undo`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "steps": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 50,
      "default": 1
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"undo","arguments":{}}`

### `capture_preview`

Render a JPEG/PNG preview of the composite (or one layer) and return it as an image so the AI can see the result.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `ps.preview`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "layer": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "id": {
          "type": "integer",
          "exclusiveMinimum": 0
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "id"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "maxSize": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 64,
      "maximum": 2048,
      "default": 1024
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "jpeg",
        "png"
      ],
      "default": "jpeg"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

## 16. Export & delivery

### `export_png`

Export a PNG copy (keeps transparency).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.saveAs`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "path": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 300000
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 300000
    },
    "compression": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9,
      "default": 6
    },
    "interlaced": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "path"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"export_png","arguments":{"path":"designs/example.png"}}`

Example: `{"name":"export_transparent_image","arguments":{"path":"designs/example.png"}}`

### `export_multiple_sizes`

Export several resized copies in one call (e.g. 1080, 720, 320 wide).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.saveAs`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "baseName": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 100,
      "pattern": "^[\\w.-]+$"
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "psd",
        "psb",
        "png",
        "jpg",
        "tiff",
        "pdf",
        "webp",
        "gif",
        "bmp"
      ],
      "default": "png"
    },
    "sizes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "width": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "height": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "suffix": {
            "type": "string",
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[\\w.-]*$"
          }
        },
        "additionalProperties": false
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 30
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    },
    "quality": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "folder",
    "baseName",
    "sizes"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"export_multiple_sizes","arguments":{"folder":"designs/example.png","baseName":"Example","sizes":[]}}`

### `create_delivery_package`

Deliverables folder: validates the document, saves PSD + requested formats/sizes, and writes manifest.json listing every file with byte sizes.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** `doc.info`, `layer.list`, `doc.saveAs`

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "doc": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "integer",
              "exclusiveMinimum": 0
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "id"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 255
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "name"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      ],
      "description": "Document reference {id} or {name}. Omit to use the active document."
    },
    "folder": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1024,
      "description": "File path inside an allowed directory (see ALLOWED_PATHS)"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 100,
      "pattern": "^[\\w.-]+$"
    },
    "formats": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": [
          "png",
          "jpg",
          "webp",
          "tiff",
          "pdf"
        ]
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 5,
      "default": [
        "png",
        "jpg"
      ]
    },
    "sizes": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "minimum": 16,
        "maximum": 30000
      },
      "maxItems": 10,
      "default": [],
      "description": "Extra widths"
    },
    "includePsd": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Replace an existing file (never silent; default false)"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "folder",
    "name"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_delivery_package","arguments":{"folder":"designs/example.png","name":"Example"}}`

## 17. AI design engine

### `list_templates`

List professional design templates and supported styles.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"list_templates","arguments":{}}`

### `plan_design`

Turn a brief into a structured, validated design plan (JSON) WITHOUT touching Photoshop. Inspect or edit it, then call execute_design_plan.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "brief": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 4000,
      "description": "Natural-language design brief. Quote exact copy, e.g. headline \"PURE BEAUTY\"."
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "minimalist",
        "luxury",
        "corporate",
        "editorial",
        "modern",
        "retro",
        "swiss",
        "brutalist",
        "geometric",
        "organic",
        "playful",
        "futuristic",
        "vintage",
        "wireframe",
        "contemporary"
      ]
    },
    "template": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Template id (see list_templates)"
    },
    "copy": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "headline": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 300
        },
        "subheadline": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 500
        },
        "body": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 5000
        },
        "cta": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 100
        },
        "brand": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 100
        },
        "tagline": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 200
        },
        "contact": {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 500
        },
        "items": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "maxLength": 200
          },
          "maxItems": 20
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "baseColor": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$",
      "description": "Derive the palette from this color"
    },
    "harmony": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "monochrome",
        "analogous",
        "complementary",
        "triadic",
        "split-complementary"
      ]
    },
    "palette": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$"
      },
      "description": "Override palette roles, e.g. {background:'#E9DDCC'}"
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "width": {
          "type": "number",
          "exclusiveMinimum": 0
        },
        "height": {
          "type": "number",
          "exclusiveMinimum": 0
        },
        "units": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "pt",
            "px",
            "mm",
            "cm",
            "in",
            "pc"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "width",
        "height",
        "units"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "colorMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "RGB",
        "CMYK"
      ]
    },
    "imagePath": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Image to place (must be inside an allowed directory)"
    },
    "designSystem": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "description": "UI briefs: apply the UI/UX Pro Max product palette + font pairing matched from the brief (default: on unless style or baseColor is given)"
    },
    "execute": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "const": false,
      "default": false
    },
    "preview": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true,
      "description": "Export and attach a PNG preview after execution"
    },
    "saveAs": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Save the result as this .psd path"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "brief"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"plan_design","arguments":{"brief":"Example"}}`

### `validate_design_plan`

Validate a design plan against the schema plus semantic rules (layer references, palette references, alignment dependencies, contrast).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "plan": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {}
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "plan"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"validate_design_plan","arguments":{"plan":{}}}`

### `execute_design_plan`

Execute a stored plan (planId) or an inline plan in Photoshop: creates a new document with layers and editable objects, verifies the result by inspection, and attaches a preview.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "planId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "plan": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {}
    },
    "preview": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "saveAs": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "overwrite": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"execute_design_plan","arguments":{}}`

### `get_design_plan`

Return a stored design plan (latest version unless version is given), or list all plans when planId is omitted.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "planId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "version": {
      "type": "integer"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"get_design_plan","arguments":{}}`

### `compare_design_versions`

Diff two versions of a stored plan: added/removed/changed elements, palette and document changes.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "planId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "from": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "to": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "planId",
    "from",
    "to"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"compare_design_versions","arguments":{"planId":"Example","from":1,"to":1}}`

```json
          "maxLength": 100

          "maxLength": 500
```

### `revise_design`

Apply targeted revisions to an executed plan without rebuilding: structured updates by element name, additions, removals, palette role changes, and simple instructions ("make the headline bigger", "change background to navy", "move logo up"). Unparsed instructions are reported, not guessed.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "planId": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "instructions": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 2000
    },
    "updates": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "name": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "name"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": true
      },
      "maxItems": 200
    },
    "add": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": {}
      },
      "maxItems": 200
    },
    "remove": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "maxItems": 200
    },
    "palette": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$"
      }
    },
    "apply": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "preview": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "planId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"revise_design","arguments":{"planId":"Example"}}`

### `analyze_composition`

Analyze the active document from verified Photoshop properties: margins, alignment near-misses, spacing rhythm, type scale, hierarchy, contrast, overlap and visual balance. Returns a score and findings with suggested fixes. This is geometric analysis, not visual perception.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "artboard": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"analyze_composition","arguments":{}}`

### `critique_design`

Critique the active design: verified property analysis plus an actual exported preview image attached for the AI client's own visual assessment. The server does not perform image recognition; any visual judgement must come from the client viewing the attached image.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "artboard": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "goals": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 1000,
      "description": "What the design should achieve"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"critique_design","arguments":{}}`

### `improve_design`

Apply the automatic fixes suggested by analyze_composition (margins, alignment snaps, contrast, minimum size, hierarchy) as targeted edits, then re-analyze and report before/after scores. Use dryRun to preview the changes.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "categories": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": [
          "alignment",
          "margins",
          "typography",
          "color",
          "hierarchy"
        ]
      }
    },
    "findingIds": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "dryRun": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "preview": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "artboard": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

## 18. UI design

### `search_design_knowledge`

Search the UI/UX Pro Max design knowledge base (MIT, 192 product types, 89 styles, 74 font pairings, 120+ UX rules). Domains: product (Product types → recommended style, landing pattern, dashboard style, palette focus); color (Product-type color palettes (primary, secondary, accent, background, foreground, card, muted, border, destructive)); typography (Heading/body font pairings by mood); style (UI styles (glassmorphism, neumorphism, brutalism…) with colors, effects, best-for and do-not-use-for); reasoning (Per-product design reasoning: pattern, style priority, color/typography mood, anti-patterns); landing (Landing page patterns: section order, CTA placement, color strategy); ux (UX guidelines with do / don't and severity); app-interface (Mobile app interface rules (accessibility, touch, navigation)); chart (Chart type recommendations per data type). Use it to ground style, color, type, layout and UX decisions before designing.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "domain": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "product",
        "color",
        "typography",
        "style",
        "reasoning",
        "landing",
        "ux",
        "app-interface",
        "chart"
      ]
    },
    "query": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 2,
      "maxLength": 500,
      "description": "Keywords, e.g. \"fintech\", \"glassmorphism\", \"elegant serif\", \"touch target\""
    },
    "limit": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 20,
      "default": 5
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "domain",
    "query"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"search_design_knowledge","arguments":{"domain":"product","query":"Example"}}`

### `recommend_design_system`

Recommend a complete design system for a product brief using UI/UX Pro Max reasoning: matched product type, color palette mapped to palette roles (incl. AA-safe subtle/link/onPrimary), font pairing, UI style, landing-page pattern, key considerations and anti-patterns to avoid. Pass the result's palette to create_ui_design or the design tools.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "brief": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 3,
      "maxLength": 2000,
      "description": "e.g. \"telemedicine app for elderly patients\""
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "brief"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"recommend_design_system","arguments":{"brief":"Example"}}`

### `list_ui_components`

List the UI component library (button, input, card, navbar, tab-bar, list-item, toggle, stat, chart…) with props, the auto-layout node reference, device frame presets and default design tokens. Read this before create_ui_design.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"list_ui_components","arguments":{}}`

### `get_design_tokens`

Design system for a style: color roles (incl. AA-safe 'subtle' text color), typography roles, spacing/radius/type-size tokens and control height. Use the token keys in auto-layout nodes.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "minimalist",
        "luxury",
        "corporate",
        "editorial",
        "modern",
        "retro",
        "swiss",
        "brutalist",
        "geometric",
        "organic",
        "playful",
        "futuristic",
        "vintage",
        "wireframe",
        "contemporary"
      ],
      "default": "modern"
    },
    "platform": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "mobile",
        "web"
      ],
      "default": "mobile"
    },
    "baseColor": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$"
    },
    "harmony": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "monochrome",
        "analogous",
        "complementary",
        "triadic",
        "split-complementary"
      ]
    },
    "palette": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$"
      }
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"get_design_tokens","arguments":{}}`

### `create_ui_design`

Build a UI screen from an auto-layout tree (Figma-style frames: stacks with gap/padding/alignment, fill/hug sizing, components and design tokens). The server measures and positions everything, validates contrast, then builds editable artwork in Photoshop with a preview. Call list_ui_components first for the node and component reference. execute: false returns the computed plan only.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200,
      "default": "UI Screen"
    },
    "frame": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "iphone-15",
        "iphone-se",
        "android",
        "ipad",
        "desktop",
        "laptop"
      ],
      "description": "Device preset; or give width/height"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "number",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0
    },
    "height": {
      "type": "number",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0
    },
    "style": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "minimalist",
        "luxury",
        "corporate",
        "editorial",
        "modern",
        "retro",
        "swiss",
        "brutalist",
        "geometric",
        "organic",
        "playful",
        "futuristic",
        "vintage",
        "wireframe",
        "contemporary"
      ],
      "default": "modern"
    },
    "product": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 500,
      "description": "Product/business description; applies the UI/UX Pro Max palette and font pairing for it (see recommend_design_system)"
    },
    "baseColor": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$"
    },
    "harmony": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "monochrome",
        "analogous",
        "complementary",
        "triadic",
        "split-complementary"
      ]
    },
    "palette": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{3})$"
      },
      "description": "Override palette roles"
    },
    "tokens": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": {},
      "description": "Override design tokens (spacing, radius, fontSize, controlHeight)"
    },
    "layout": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": {}
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 50,
      "description": "Auto-layout root nodes; usually one vertical stack with width/height 'fill'"
    },
    "elements": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": {}
      },
      "maxItems": 500,
      "description": "Extra absolutely-positioned plan elements drawn beneath the layout"
    },
    "execute": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "preview": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "saveAs": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layout"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"create_ui_design","arguments":{"layout":[]}}`

### `rename_layers_semantically`

Rename generic objects ("<Path>", "Rectangle 3", unnamed text) in the active document to meaningful names inferred from verified geometry and content, e.g. "Background", "Header Bar", "Button / Sign in", "Heading / Pure Beauty". dryRun returns suggestions only. Inspects top-level objects of each layer.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "artboard": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "all": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false,
      "description": "Also rename objects that already have custom names"
    },
    "dryRun": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"rename_layers_semantically","arguments":{}}`

## 19. Background jobs

### `get_job`

Status of a background job (queued | running | succeeded | failed | cancelled). When it has succeeded, also returns the tool's full result, including any preview image.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "description": "Id returned by a tool called with background: true"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "jobId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"get_job","arguments":{"jobId":"Example"}}`

### `wait_for_job`

Wait up to timeoutSeconds for a background job to finish, then return it as get_job does. If it is still running, call again; the job keeps going either way.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "description": "Id returned by a tool called with background: true"
    },
    "timeoutSeconds": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 55,
      "default": 30
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "jobId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"wait_for_job","arguments":{"jobId":"Example"}}`

### `list_jobs`

List recent background jobs, newest first (results omitted; use get_job).

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** read-only
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {},
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"list_jobs","arguments":{}}`

### `cancel_job`

Cancel a queued background job. A job already running inside Photoshop cannot be interrupted; the result says so.

- **Status:** supported
- **Kind:** write
- **Photoshop implementation (JSX bridge ops):** none (server-side)

<details><summary>Input schema</summary>

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "jobId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "description": "Id returned by a tool called with background: true"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "jobId"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
</details>

Example: `{"name":"cancel_job","arguments":{"jobId":"Example"}}`
